## Monaco Grand Prix Rooftop Lifestyle Portrait Prompt

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "subject": {
    "person": "{argument name="person" default="Young woman, early 20s, Caucasian"}",
    "hair": "{argument name="hair" default="Long, voluminous, tousled wavy blonde with lighter highlights, framing the face"}",
    "pose": "{argument name="pose" default="Sitting comfortably on a black cushioned ledge, body angled slightly left, looking thoughtfully off-camera right"}",
    "expression": "{argument name="expression" default="Gentle, calm, slight Mona Lisa smile"}"
  },
  "outfit": {
    "top": "{argument name="top" default="White structured corset-style top with thin spaghetti straps and boning details"}",
    "bottoms": "{argument name="bottoms" default="High-waisted beige utility cargo pants with large side pockets"}",
    "accessories": "{argument name="accessories" default="Small patterned Dior Saddle bag under right arm, dainty necklace, rings on left hand, painted white nails"}"
  },
  "action": {
    "hands": "{argument name="hands" default="Left hand holds a stemmed glass of rosé by the stem; right hand rests flat on the ledge behind her hip"}"
  },
  "location": {
    "setting": "{argument name="setting" default="Rooftop terrace overlooking the Fairmont Hairpin turn of the Monaco Grand Prix in Monte Carlo"}",
    "background": "{argument name="background" default="Dense hillside with multi-story buildings, hotels, a prominent beige skyscraper, construction cranes, green trees, and distant mountains under a cloudy sky. Racetrack with red/white kerbing and vehicles below."}"
  },
  "typography": {
    "text": "{argument name="typography" default="aramco, UBS, Heineken 0.0, MONTE-CARLO, CAFE DE PARIS on racetrack advertising barriers"}"
  },
  "lighting": {
    "type": "{argument name="lighting type" default="Natural daylight, overcast afternoon"}",
    "quality": "{argument name="lighting quality" default="Soft, diffused, flat, shadowless, slightly hazy"}"
  },
  "composition": {
    "style": "{argument name="composition style" default="Candid lifestyle photograph, slightly low angle, medium shot, deep depth of field, asymmetrical balance, 4:5 portrait ratio"}",
    "color_palette": "{argument name="color palette" default="Muted, earthy, overcast tones; low-medium contrast; moderately desaturated"}"
  }
}
```

## 8K Ultra-Realistic Kashmir Winter Scene Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A cinematic, ultra-realistic winter scene in {argument name="location" default="Kashmir"} during {argument name="period" default="Chillai Kalan"}. Heavy snowfall blankets traditional {argument name="building type" default="wooden houses"}, rooftops covered in thick ice and snow. Frost clings to windows, icicles hanging from eaves. Kashmiri people dressed in traditional woolen {argument name="clothing" default="pherans"} stand outdoors and near doorways, holding glowing {argument name="prop" default="kangri fire pots"} close to keep warm. Visible breath in the freezing air, snowflakes falling steadily. Moody overcast sky, cold {argument name="color palette" default="blue-white color palette"} with warm {argument name="light color" default="orange glow"} from the kangri fires. Hyper-detailed textures of snow, ice, fabric, and skin. Dramatic cinematic lighting, shallow depth of field, realistic weather effects, winter storm atmosphere. 8K resolution, ultra-sharp focus, photorealistic, documentary-style promotional realism.
```

## Douyin Luxury Night Portrait Prompt

> Lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Subject]
person = {argument name="person" default="Woman"}
facial_reference = {argument name="facial reference" default="EXACT_MATCH"}
hair_style = {argument name="hair style" default="Long, straight, brown"}
hair_action = {argument name="hair action" default="Raising arm to tuck hair behind ear"}
accessories = {argument name="accessories" default="Black sunglasses (worn in hair), black chain bag"}
outfit_base = {argument name="outfit base" default="Short black dress"}
outfit_outer = {argument name="outfit outer" default="Cropped beige faux fur jacket (long/thick fur)"}
legwear = {argument name="legwear" default="Sheer tights"}

[Environment]
location_type = {argument name="location type" default="European-style elegant buildings"}
time_of_day = {argument name="time of day" default="Night"}
lighting_style = {argument name="lighting style" default="Douyin-style soft white glow"}
lighting_temp = {argument name="lighting temp" default="Cool tones with warm highlights"}
atmosphere = {argument name="atmosphere" default="Ethereal, luxury, dreamy, elegant"}
main_object = {argument name="main object" default="White luxury car"}
interaction = {argument name="interaction" default="Leaning confidently on car"}

[CameraSettings]
model = {argument name="camera model" default="Canon EOS R5"}
lens = {argument name="lens" default="50mm f/1.2"}
aperture = {argument name="aperture" default="f/1.8"}
iso = {argument name="iso" default="800"}
shutter_speed = {argument name="shutter speed" default="1/160s"}
white_balance = {argument name="white balance" default="4200K"}
focus = {argument name="focus" default="Sharp on subject"}
depth_of_field = {argument name="depth of field" default="Shallow (cinematic bokeh)"}

[PostProcessing]
skin_texture = {argument name="skin texture" default="Porcelain-white, luminous, smooth"}
aesthetic = {argument name="aesthetic" default="Douyin luxury"}
highlight_behavior = {argument name="highlight behavior" default="Slightly overexposed"}
visual_effects = {argument name="visual effects" default="Reflective car light effects, soft white glow"}
```

## Vectorized Profile Portrait with Textural Detail

> Minimal manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Close-up, high-detail portrait of an individual (face shape exactly the same as reference) rendered in a vectorized style with flat, saturated color blocking. The individual is in profile, looking right, with a serious expression. They wear small, round black sunglasses pushed down to reveal the eye. Outfit is a dark {argument name="jacket color" default="forest green/teal"} high-collared jacket. Lighting is bright, clean, and dramatic, originating from the front-right, creating sharp, well-defined shadows. Solid deep {argument name="background color" default="burgundy-red"} background instead of teal. High resolution, extreme detail, vertical orientation, clear edges.
```

## Sci-Fi Book Cover Diorama Grid Prompt

> Minimal, fütüristik 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
2x2 grid, clean high-end studio, sharp micro-detail, consistent angle, shallow depth of field. Panel A: “{argument name="Book 1 Title" default="Dune"}” as a sand-and-brass kinetic sculpture inside an open book: a sandworm form implied by moving dunes, tiny stillsuit figure, shimmering sand grains. Panel B: “{argument name="Book 2 Title" default="Foundation"}” as an art-deco “psychohistory” device: translucent rings, tiny city skyline, rotating predictive dials embedded into the book cover. Panel C: “{argument name="Book 3 Title" default="Hyperion"}” as a reliquary diorama: time-tomb geometry, thorn-metal filigree, tiny pilgrims as figurines on a path across pages. Panel D: “{argument name="Book 4 Title" default="Ender’s Game"}” as a zero-gravity battle-room cube diorama: floating miniature soldiers and blocks suspended by nearly invisible threads, crisp reflections, futuristic matte metal.
```

## Weekly Outfit Coordination Lookbook Generation

> Minimal, stüdyo ışıklı moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
# Settings (Change here)
Settings:
[Character Traits]: {argument name="Character Traits" default="High school girl with long black hair"}
[Fashion Theme]: {argument name="Fashion Theme" default="Modern high school girl's casual wear, neat and clean style"}

Conversion Rules:
- An image of the same person with [Character Traits] lined up horizontally in a fashion lookbook of 7 people.
- Wearing only 7 different new casual outfits based on the [Fashion Theme].
- Completely maintaining the [Art Style/Style] of the reference image.
- Not wearing the [Reference Image Outfit], but wearing 7 different outfits based on the [Fashion Theme].
- Full body shot.
- The background is pure white, or slightly ivory.
- Natural shadows fall at the feet. The image layout is like a magazine clipping.
- The words "Monday", "Tuesday", "Wednesday", "Thursday", "Friday", "Saturday", "Sunday" are written under each person, in order from left.
- Stylish title text at the top.

Art Style:
- High resolution
- Studio lighting
- 4k
- --ar 16:9
```

## Culinary Origin Miniature Kitchen Display

> Lüks yemek görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
do this for {argument name="dish name" default="eggs benedict"} :

  <instructions>
Input A is a famous dish (photo or dish name).
Analyze the cuisine's origin country, there person who invented it or said to have, traditional preparation, cultural significance, and key ingredients.
Optional Input B is a style reference (culinary museum / artisan packaging). If missing, choose Michelin-star plating aesthetic meets toy packaging.
Goal: Premium clear acrylic box containing a miniature kitchen scene with the figurine of the person who invented it preparing the dish, surrounded by ingredient compartments like a high-end spice collection.
Rules:
- Center: tiny kitchen setup with chef mid-preparation
- 10-15 individual compartments with micro versions of each ingredient
- Include miniature cooking tools specific to the dish
- Add a tiny "origin map" showing the dish's birthplace
- Packaging uses colors from the cuisine's flag or traditional pottery
- Label with dish name in both English and origin language, show the date it first started
Output: ONE image, 4:5 artisan food photography style
</instructions>
```

## Composite Racing Car Shot with Vintage Film Branding

> Retro görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Medium Shot (MS): Central mass of these exact three racing cars captured from a slightly elevated angle, showcasing their distinctive branding liveries, sponsor logos (Polaroid, Sony, Kodak), and detailed body modifications. Harsh direct flash illuminates their wet surfaces, creating dramatic reflections on the rain-soaked Canggu street. Motion-blurred scooters and pedestrians frame the edges. Cropped composition with intentional cut-off at edges. High contrast between illuminated car bodies and pitch-black background. Disposable camera aesthetic with slightly grainy texture and uneven exposure.
```

## Diverse Fashion Editorial Grid

> Retro moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
6x6 grid layout. Editorial fashion photography. Use the reference clothing item on all 36 models. Every single cell must feature a completely unique individual—no two people should look remotely similar.
Maximize human diversity across:
Age: Teenagers, 20s, 30s, 40s, 50s, 60s, 70s, 80s+, elderly with wrinkles and grey hair
Body types: Extremely thin, slim, athletic, muscular, stocky, chubby, fat, plus-size, obese, petite, tall, short, broad-shouldered, narrow-framed, pear-shaped, apple-shaped, hourglass, rectangular, long-limbed, short-limbed.
Ethnicity & skin tones: East Asian, Southeast Asian, South Asian, Middle Eastern, North African, Sub-Saharan African, West African, East African, Indigenous Australian, Pacific Islander, Native American, Indigenous South American, Scandinavian, Mediterranean, Eastern European, Western European, Caribbean, mixed race, albino. Full spectrum of skin tones from the palest to the deepest.
Hair: Bald, shaved, buzz cut, afro, locs, braids, straight, wavy, curly, coily, long, short, grey, white, natural colors, receding hairline, thinning hair, thick full hair, hijab, head wraps, turbans.
Features: Freckles, vitiligo, acne, scars, birthmarks, moles, gap teeth, crooked teeth, big nose, small nose, wide nose, narrow nose, round face, angular face, soft jawline, strong jawline, monolid eyes, deep-set eyes, hooded eyes, big lips, thin lips, visible disabilities, prosthetics, wheelchair users, amputees.
Each cell: different pose, different camera angle. Poses should feel editorial and unexpected—leaning, crouching, mid-stride, arched, sprawled, seated, jumping, twisting, lounging. Camera angles vary—low angle, overhead, Dutch tilt, wide shot, tight crop, ground-level, profile, three-quarter view.
Moody, high contrast, cinematic lighting. Varied environments—studio, architectural, urban, natural. Sharp detail, clothing as focal point. Final result should feel like the most inclusive, human-rich fashion editorial ever created—a true celebration of every single type of human body that exists.
```

## Object-Based Miniature Surreal Ecosystem

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create an ultra-detailed, miniature, surreal world built entirely around the object shown in the uploaded image. Design tiny, lifelike figures that interact naturally and logically with the object as if it is their complete environment. Every element of architecture, terrain, tools, textures, and lighting must adapt organically to the object’s real physical form, materials, and surface characteristics.

Avoid any preconceived interpretations; instead, let the object’s geometry dictate the world’s design. Rails, optics, grooves, screws, edges, cavities, and mechanical components should naturally define pathways, platforms, work zones, and scale. The surrounding organic elements visible in the image should influence how the miniature world integrates with the object, creating a believable coexistence between natural textures and engineered surfaces.

Ensure the figures’ interactions feel grounded and observational, subtly emphasizing structure, material weight, and mechanical precision without dramatization, glorification, or promotion. The scene should feel physically plausible, quiet, and lived-in.

Render with photorealistic depth, cinematic macro photography, shallow depth of field, micro-details, soft volumetric lighting, and seamless integration between characters and the object. The final composition should feel like a believable, tiny ecosystem existing on and within the object itself.

Aspect ratio 16:9.
```

```
{
  "image_layout": "2x2 grid collage featuring four distinct photographs of the same female subject.",
  "subject_general": {
    "gender": "Female",
    "hair_color": "Dark brown/black",

## Interactive Billboard Selfie Prompt (Jim Carrey Style)

> Lüks afiş üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "project_name": "{argument name="project name" default="Pro_Billboard_Interactive_Lifestyle_v9"}",
  "usage_guide": "1. Fill in STAR_NAME, STAR_EXPRESSION, USER_POSE. 2. Set Aspect Ratio (e.g., 3:4). 3. Upload FULL BODY reference. 4. This version creates a casual, fun interaction between you and the giant poster.",
  "prompts": {
    "positive_prompt": "A high-end editorial fashion shot, full body, captured in {argument name="aspect ratio" default="3:4"} format.\
\
[Spatial Logic – Perfect Depth]\
A real person stands in the foreground, positioned about {argument name="distance" default="50cm"} (a few dozen centimeters) away from the background wall. Not touching the wall. There is a visible, natural depth. Soft shadows are cast on the wall behind, proving the physical separation.\
\
[Subject – Casual & Interactive]\
Subject's outfit, gender, and age perfectly match the uploaded reference image. Full body visible from head to toe, including shoes.\
POSE & VIBE: The person is {argument name="user pose" default="pointing at the billboard, laughing loudly, excited"}. The pose is casual, unposed, and lifestyle-oriented (NOT a stiff passport photo). It feels like a fun street snap where the person is interacting with the background celebrity.\
\
[Background – Giant Expressive Poster]\
Behind the person is a massive, building-sized commercial advertisement. It features a gigantic portrait of {argument name="star name" default="Thomas Stanley Holland"} with a {argument name="star expression" default="seductive charming smile"} expression. Visible from the shoulders up, looking huge and imposing.\
\
[Texture & Material Reality]\
The billboard has a realistic heavy-duty vinyl or canvas texture. Visible details: slight surface ripples, tension wrinkles, uneven lighting reflection, and micro-folds. It looks like a real physical object, not a smooth screen.\
\
[Lighting]\
Soft overcast natural light, 8k resolution, raw photo quality, sharp focus.",
    "negative_prompt": "stiff pose, military stance, passport photo, bored expression, touching the wall, zero distance, flat digital image, perfectly smooth texture, glossy screen, fake poster, floating feet, missing shoes, cropped, lowres, bad anatomy, changing outfit, cartoon, 3d render"
  }
}
```

## Personalized Mural Studio Portrait

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a high-resolution studio photograph featuring THE_SUBJECT, using the uploaded photo as a strict visual reference for identity. THE_SUBJECT’s facial features, skin tone, body proportions, hairstyle, facial hair, and overall appearance must match the reference image exactly, with no reinterpretation or stylization of THE_SUBJECT. 

THE_SUBJECT is standing naturally and casually, leaning against a plain concrete or plaster studio wall. THE_SUBJECT is photographed as a real person in a real studio environment — this is a photographic image, not an illustration.

Painted directly onto the wall behind THE_SUBJECT is a large-scale mural depicting THE_SUBJECT’s face and upper body. The mural is a stylized vector-art portrait of THE_SUBJECT, clearly recognizable as the same person, rendered in bold graphic shapes, clean edges, and modern illustrative color blocking. The mural is significantly larger than life-size and occupies most of the wall surface.

THE_SUBJECT’s real body partially overlaps and obscures the mural, reinforcing depth and scale, while still making it immediately clear that the mural represents THE_SUBJECT. The mural must remain visibly painted on the wall, not floating, not projected, and not a digital overlay.

Lighting is controlled studio lighting with a cinematic feel: soft but directional, illuminating both THE_SUBJECT and the mural evenly, with subtle contrast and color separation that allows the real subject and the painted mural to coexist clearly in the same physical space.

The final image should feel like a professional studio portrait photographed in front of a custom mural wall — realistic, grounded, and visually cohesive — with a clear distinction between the real human subject and the stylized painted portrait behind them.
```

## 16K Cinematic Night Portrait with Strict Identity Lock

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "type": "image_generation_prompt",
  "style": "16K ultra-high-resolution, hyper-realistic, cinematic, premium realism",

  "identity_preservation": {
    "use_uploaded_image": true,
    "alter_face": false,
    "notes": "Strict identity lock. Preserve exact facial structure, skin tone, beard shape, eye placement, and hairline. Face must remain fully recognizable and natural. No beautification."
  },

  "subject": {
    "gender": "male",
    "pose": "standing naturally, relaxed and confident",
    "expression": "subtle half-smile, calm, approachable"
  },

  "wardrobe": {
    "outerwear": "tailored dark jacket or overshirt in black, charcoal, or deep navy",
    "inner_layer": "minimal plain tee or knit",
    "fit": "clean, structured, premium",
    "branding": "no logos"
  },

  "environment": {
    "location": "modern urban night setting",
    "background": "softly blurred city lights and skyline, believable and present-day",
    "air": "clear night air, no fog or haze"
  },

  "lighting": {
    "setup": "cinematic night lighting with soft frontal key light and subtle rim light",
    "color_tone": "cool cyan and teal with very subtle magenta accents",
    "characteristics": "balanced exposure, deep controlled shadows, no glow"
  },

  "camera": {
    "lens": "85mm cinematic portrait lens",
    "framing": "chest-up portrait",
    "depth_of_field": "soft background separation",
    "focus": "tack-sharp on face"
  },
  
  "quality": {
    "realism": "ultra-realistic",
    "skin": "natural pores and micro-texture",
    "materials": "physically accurate fabric and lighting interaction"
  },

  "mood": "quiet luxury, relaxed confidence, cinematic",

  "output_goal": "A 16K ultra-realistic cinematic night portrait that feels like a high-end luxury brand campaign while perfectly preserving the subject’s exact identity."
}
```

## Tang Dynasty Empress in Paris Fashion Magazine Cover Prompt

> Moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Generate a 9:16 vertical, realistic photo of 'Prosperous Tang Dynasty meets Paris': Use the uploaded FACE_REF as the character identity reference, 100% restoring the facial features and bone structure; the character is wearing a heavy-duty court gown in the style of a 'Tang Dynasty (High Tang) Imperial Concubine' (Qixiong Shanqun, large sleeves, peony patterns, gold ornaments), photographed at Trocadéro Square or the banks of the Seine beneath the Eiffel Tower in Paris. The image should have the photography, styling, and cover design quality of a COSMO fashion magazine, maintaining the magazine's consistent design style (English design).

【INPUTS | Input】 FACE_REF: The uploaded reference image for the character's facial features (Identity Lock)

【ABSOLUTE PRIORITY | Identity Lock (Highest Priority)】 100% restoration of FACE_REF's facial features and bone structure: Face shape, appearance, eye distance, nose bridge/alae, lip shape, jawline, and cheekbone structure must be completely consistent, without drifting. Realistic skin texture: Visible subtle textures and pores, avoid excessive smoothing or 'influencer' style. Adult female image.

【SHOT TYPE | Choose one of two shots (randomly selected by the model, must be truly consistent)】 A) Bust Cover: From the chest to above the waist, the headwear and upper body embroidery are clearest, eyes are sharpest focus (Primary recommendation) B) Full Body Cover: Complete presentation of the layered gown and skirt patterns from head to toe, stronger perspective of the Eiffel Tower's steel structure (Alternative) (Regardless of A/B: must maintain 'cover composition', with white space for layout, but no torn paper/hand-drawn effects.)

【LOCATION | Scene (Eiffel Tower, Paris)】 Eiffel Tower, Paris: Classic steel structure tower background. Choose one of two weather conditions (random): 1) Rainy Paris (ground slightly wet, soft light, high-end gray tone, strong fashion sense) 2) Brilliant Dusk (sunset glow, golden light and shadow, romantic atmosphere) Clean background: No tourists, no modern signs, no phone status bar UI, no watermarks/subtitles, no railings.

【LIGHTING & CAMERA | Photography Quality (Magazine Level)】 Lens feel: 85mm portrait quality (bust) or 50mm/70mm (full body), shallow depth of field, eyes in sharpest focus. Lighting: Natural light (emphasizing ambient light) + cinematic fill light; gold ornaments have realistic highlights but are not blown out; silk luster is clearly discernible. Color: High-end cinematic feel, French beige/blue-gray tones form a strong visual tension with the Tang Dynasty clothing's 'red, green, and gold' color scheme; overall clean, transparent, and rich in texture.

【WARDROBE | Tang Dynasty Court Gown (Plump, Flowing, Intense Colors)】 Target aesthetic: 'Ornate gold ornaments, facial makeup (mianye huadian), Qixiong skirt, flowing pibo (long scarf), intense red, green, and gold, extremely flamboyant.'

Silhouette and Layers (Must reflect Tang Dynasty characteristics) Inner layer: Hezi (bustier), exposing the neck and upper chest skin (reflecting the open atmosphere of the Tang Dynasty) Middle layer: Duijin short jacket (straight collar), transparent and light material (gauze/silk) Outer layer: Daxiu Shan (extremely wide sleeves, dragging on the floor), spread out like wings Decoration: Pibo (long silk ribbon), wrapped around the arms, fluttering in the wind Bottom wear: Qixiong Poqun (skirt waist tied at the chest, wide skirt hem, multi-color splicing or printing) Fabric and Craftsmanship (Heavy craftsmanship must be 'visible') Main fabrics: Silk, brocade, gold-printed gauze (combination of lightness and heaviness) Main techniques: Printing and dyeing (wax resist/clamping), gold thread embroidery, gold weaving Pattern themes (suitable for High Tang): Baoxiang flower, continuous peony vine, auspicious beasts (lion/kylin), Tuanhua (giant, full patterns) Details: Huadian (red decoration on the forehead), Mianye (red dots at the corners of the mouth), Armband (metal ornament on the upper arm) Headwear (Opulent Tang Dynasty headwear) Core structure: High bun (Yunji/Zhuaimaji), adorned with large peony flowers (real or silk) Decoration: Gold comb back (gold comb inserted into the bun), Buyao (swaying when moving), gold hairpin Earrings: Precious gold and jade

【COLOR MATRIX | Color Matrix Pairing (Must be executed, and must be 'Intense Tang Style')】 (Adjusted to Tang Dynasty popular colors: red-green contrast, brilliant gold) Randomly select 1 set from the following three 'Main Color Schemes' for the [Base Color/Large Area Main Fabric Color].

Scheme G (Stone Blue/Emerald Green Base): Base Color: {argument name="base color 1" default="Emerald Green/Stone Blue"} (common intermediate color) Embroidery/Printing: Madder Red, Gold, Orange Yellow, Off-White Scheme A (Peony Red Base): Base Color: {argument name="base color 2" default="Peony Red/Pomegranate Red"} (most classic Tang style) Embroidery/Printing: Gold, Emerald Green, Sapphire Blue, Purple Scheme R (Purple Base): Base Color: {argument name="base color 3" default="Purple/Grape Color"} (noble) Embroidery/Printing: Gold, Vermilion, Grass Green, Silver

【Color Execution Rules】 Color contrast must be strong, reflecting the grandeur of the High Tang, but the overall tone must be unified in a high-end feel.

【POSE | Cover Pose (Confident, Expansive, Queenly Feel)】 Bust: Head slightly raised, eyes looking down, one hand gently touching the bun or pibo Full Body: Arms spread out to drive the Daxiu Shan and Pibo, showcasing the volume of the clothing, full aura Expression: Confident, flamboyant, charming, generous; not shy.

【OUTPUT | Output】 1 vertical 9:16 realistic magazine cover-level photo Random: Bust or Full Body (A/B choice) Random: Rainy or Dusk (Weather choice) Random: Emerald Green / Peony Red / Purple (Color matrix choice) Styling: Tang Dynasty court gown direction + Peony flower/Gold comb headwear + Qixiong Daxiu + Pibo
```

## Vintage Action Figure Packaging Generator

> Fotogerçekçi, retro illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
<instructions> 

1. Convert the subject's skin and clothing into semi-gloss ABS plastic textures. Visible joints at elbows and knees.
2. Analyze the subject's profession or hobby (or infer it). Generate 4 distinct "accessories" included in the bubble (e.g., a tiny plastic laptop, a sword, a coffee mug).
3. Determine if the vibe is 80s (Neon/He-Man), 90s (Grungy/Spawn), or 70s (Kenner/Star Wars), 2000 (appropriate style) or 2025 (appropriate style).

Phase 2: The Packaging Design
1. Cardback Art:The cardboard backing features a dynamic 2D illustration of the subject in an action pose behind the plastic bubble.
2. Wear & Tear: The card corners must be slightly frayed/white. The plastic bubble should have minor scratches or yellowing to simulate age.
3. Typography: Bold, explosive comic book fonts. Title: "THE [Subject's Defining Trait]".

Phase 3: The Shot
A slightly high-angle studio shot of the unpromoted toy resting on a wooden table or carpet, as if just found at a garage sale.
</instruction>

A photorealistic macro shot of a vintage action figure mint-on-card. The figure inside the yellowed plastic blister pack is the input subject turned into a plastic toy with visible articulation joints. The cardboard backing features retro character art. Worn edges, price tag sticker residue. 2025 toy aesthetic
```

## Intimate POV Foot Care Scene

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyper-realistic, intimate POV shot from the perspective of the foot care provider. The frame is dominated by the client's beautiful, relaxed feet resting gently in your gloved hands. The focus is on the details: the soft arch, the neatly trimmed nails awaiting polish, the smooth, toned skin of her calves and ankles glistening with a light layer of aromatic oil or warm water.
The woman herself is partially visible in the top of the frame—her bare legs extending into the distance, the hem of a luxurious {argument name="robe color" default="cream"} silk robe draped across her thighs. Her posture is one of complete surrender; her head is tilted back against the plush headrest of the spa chair, eyes closed in deep relaxation, a faint, contented smile on her lips.
The setting is a calm, elegant outdoor cabana. Soft, diffused natural light from a setting sun filters through white linen curtains, creating a warm, golden glow on her skin. On the marble workstation within the frame: a small bowl of warm water with floating {argument name="flower type" default="jasmine"} petals, a precise nail file, a bottle of {argument name="nail polish color" default="deep burgundy"} nail polish, and a soft towel.
Shot with a macro lens (like a 100mm f/2.8) to capture stunning, tack-sharp detail—the tiny beads of water, the texture of the towel, the dewy skin—while beautifully blurring the background into a dreamy, creamy bokeh of light and greenery. The atmosphere is serene, sensual, and focused on the intimate, trusting act of care.
```

A masterpiece, raw, authentic, 8K cinematic lifestyle portrait, executed in contemporary cinematic realism and luxury lifestyle photography with a Scandinavian minimalist sensibility, capturing a calm, intimate, and quietly luxurious self-care moment. THE_SUBJECT, exactly as shown in the reference image and maintaining 100% fidelity to identity, facial structure, expression, hairstyle, body proportions, posture, and overall physical presence, is reclined lengthwise in a modern bathtub, resting naturally along its full length rather than upright. Legs extend outward and rest along the edge of the tub, with the foot positioned closest to the camera.

The camera is placed low at the far end of the bathtub, aligned roughly at THE_SUBJECT’s eye level, creating a cinematic perspective with natural foreshortening. The extended leg and foot appear larger in the foreground due to perspective, while the torso and head recede gently into the frame, enhancing depth and visual interest without distortion. The composition feels intimate, and deliberately natural.

THE_SUBJECT is wearing {argument name="headphone model" default="Beats Studio Pro over-ear wireless headphones"} in {argument name="headphone color" default="sandstone color"} (as shown in the reference image) and is fully absorbed in the moment, ignoring the camera. There are no visible garments; THE_SUBJECT’s body is submerged beneath warm bathwater and soft foam, with only the head, arms, and legs visible above the surface. The bath bubbles and steam tastefully obscure the body, maintaining a refined, editorial tone with no explicit anatomy visible.

The modern bathroom environment features neutral stone tiles, soft bathroom slippers on the floor, and a large contemporary wall-mounted abstract art piece composed of layered, organic forms in muted stone, sand, and soft aqua tones. Neatly arranged bottles rest on a minimal shelf, and the space is enveloped in a warm, steamy, gently humid early-evening atmosphere. Environmental elements may vary slightly as long as they remain consistent with an upscale, contemporary interior and do not affect subject accuracy.

The scene is illuminated by diffused natural window light combined with warm wall-mounted lamps, producing soft, enveloping illumination and gentle, low-contrast shadows. The image uses a shallow depth of field ({argument name="lens focal length" default="50mm prime"}, {argument name="aperture" default="f/2.8"}), a cinematic foreground-to-background composition adapted to foreshortened perspective, fine film grain, subtle digital noise, mild lens bloom, and atmospheric humidity. – Vertical framing

Sensual, elegant, modern Christmas, minimalist luxury, woman-as-light, glow, power, and contemporary sophistication.

## Vintage Action Figure Toyification Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, retro illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
<instruction>
**Phase 1: Toyification**
1. **Plasticity:** Convert the subject's skin and clothing into semi-gloss ABS plastic textures. Visible joints at elbows and knees.
2. **Accessory** Analyze the subject's profession or hobby (or infer it). Generate 2 distinct "accessories" included in the bubble (e.g., a tiny plastic laptop, a sword, a coffee mug).
3. **Era:** Determine if the vibe is 80s (Neon/He-Man), 90s (Grungy/Spawn), or 70s (Kenner/Star Wars).

**Phase 2: Packaging Design**
1. **Cardback Art:** The cardboard backing features a dynamic 2D illustration of the subject in an action pose behind the plastic bubble.
2. **Wear & Tear:** The card corners must be slightly frayed/white. The plastic bubble should have minor scratches or yellowing to simulate age.
3. **Typography:** Bold, explosive comic book fonts. Title: "the [Subject's Defining Trait]".

**Phase 3: The Shot**
A slightly high-angle studio shot of the unpromoted toy resting on a wooden table or carpet, as if just found at a garage sale.
</instruction>

A photorealistic macro shot of a vintage action figure mint-on-card. The figure inside the yellowed plastic blister pack is the input subject turned into a plastic toy with visible articulation joints. The cardboard backing features retro character art. Worn edges, price tag sticker residue. 1990s toy aesthetic.
```

A vibrant, full-body shot, captured in a soft, vintage-style photograph, features a young woman with long, dark, wavy hair sitting casually on a wooden chair with metal legs. She wears a white t-shirt with a red graphic print layered under a full-length red jumpsuit with long sleeves, covering her from neck to toe. She still wears white knee-high lace socks with frilly tops and red sneakers with white logos for added retro charm. Her left leg is bent, with her foot resting on the chair, while her right leg extends forward.

Her makeup is distinctive, with heavily flushed pink cheeks and nose, winged eyeliner, and glossy lips. Small red bows sit in her hair, and her fingernails are painted blue. In her left hand, she holds a vintage green and black point-and-shoot camera resting on her knee. Her right hand is raised, with her fingers touching her temple, giving a thoughtful or relaxed pose.

The background includes a light yellow wall and a window with a sheer white curtain. To her right, a vintage-style “Canister” jar sits on the windowsill. A plant is visible in the lower left corner. The lighting resembles a direct flash, creating bright, slightly harsh illumination on the subject, highlighting her skin and clothing textures while casting sharp shadows. The warm, saturated color grading gives the image a playful, retro, dreamy aesthetic reminiscent of grainy film photography.

Use photo reference for facial features.
```

## Advanced Geolocation and Model Generation Prompts

> Manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
【Prompt (Mannequin Pose)】
Divide the screen into 6 equal parts and create genderless mannequins (without clothes or accessories) using 6 figure images, each with a different composition and angle.

【Prompt (Model Only Generation)】
Divide the screen into 6 equal parts and execute the prompt with 6 compositions and pose images of a female model in a white area. Upscale the skin texture to the resolution height of the female model so that the skin quality is visible.

【Prompt (Model and Scene Generation)】
Divide the screen into 6 equal parts and execute the prompt with 6 compositions and pose images of a female model and a white area. Do not use the blue-back background in the left area as is; faithfully reproduce the scenery of the location. ## Hyper-Realistic Reflection Portrait Prompt

This is a highly realistic, tightly composed image showing a fair-skinned woman with flawless skin reflected on an iPad screen placed on an airplane folding tray table, with a pair of sunglasses serving as a foreground accent. The iPad is surrounded by beige-gray cabin seats. On the iPad screen, the woman is semi-reclining, resting her left hand on her chin, fingers lightly touching her temple, in a relaxed posture. She is taking a photo with an {argument name="Phone Model" default="iPhone 16 Pro Max"} in her right hand, with part of her face appearing on the tablet screen. Her expression is gentle, her gaze slightly downward, giving a dreamy and tranquil feeling.

She is wearing an oversized {argument name="Top Color" default="beige"} hoodie, the fabric thick and soft, with the hood pulled up. She wears simple silver rings on her wrist, and her nails have a French manicure. Her hair is {argument name="Hair Color" default="dark brown"} with subtle highlights, long and straight, layered, fluffy and full, with a center-parted fringe gently covering her forehead to frame her face. Her skin is fair and translucent, flawless, with delicate Korean-style light makeup. Her cheeks have a faint blush, her lips are coated with dewy rose-pink lipstick, and her eyeliner is thin, with curled lashes.

Through the reflection, the environment visible is simple yet vibrant: the interior of a premium/business class airplane cabin. The wide porthole reflects the soft blue sky light outside the window. The iPad's camera interface—shutter button, small icons, and dock—is clearly visible on the screen.

Soft, warm pastel tones. Edited in a “Korean Soft Light” style. 9:16 vertical aspect ratio, high definition, high quality. **Do not change the face.
```

## Cinematic Aircraft Carrier Pilot Portrait (Identity Lock)

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-realistic 8K portrait of a female model in her twenties, identity preservation is ABSOLUTE with 100% facial and body alignment. The model is seated in the cockpit of an aircraft carrier, high above the vast, deep blue ocean. The backdrop features expansive sky with a soft gradient of clouds, the endless ocean stretching out behind her. The model wears a glossy white halter crop top, black faux leather choker with a metallic lock, matching black cuffs on her wrists, and a glossy black pilot-style cap with a metallic winged heart emblem. The wind is softly blowing, creating gentle movement in her long, voluminous pink hair.

Mood and Concept: Bold, cinematic, and adventurous with a hint of rebellious energy. The setting evokes a sense of power, freedom, and adrenaline. Her posture is strong and confident, seated comfortably in the cockpit, with one hand resting on the armrest and the other on her lap, showcasing the sleekness of the outfit and accessories. The ocean breeze subtly lifts her hair, adding a sense of movement and life to the scene.

Outfit and Styling:
Glossy white halter crop top, tight fit, accentuating her toned midriff.
Black faux leather choker-style collar with a subtle silver lock at the front.
Matching black cuffs on both wrists with a polished, reflective finish.
Glossy black cap with a shiny metallic winged heart emblem on the front.

Hair and Makeup:
Hair: Soft, voluminous pink hair in long, tousled waves, subtly lifted by the sea breeze. The hair has a glossy sheen, with some strands gently flowing, adding a sense of realism and movement.

Makeup: Bold, anime-inspired makeup with dramatic eyeliner and large, doe-like eyes. The eyebrows are defined with a strong arch. Her lips are painted in a soft, glossy pink MLBB shade to complement her vibrant hair color, giving her an ethereal, almost otherworldly vibe. Skin has a porcelain, flawless finish.

Photography Specs:
Canon EOS R5 with 85mm f1.2 lens.
Aperture f1.4 for a shallow depth of field and soft bokeh background.
Shutter speed 1/250 s.
ISO 200 for perfect exposure in daylight with the soft sea breeze lighting.
Aspect Ratio 5:7

Detail and Finish:
Soft rim light tracing her face and highlighting the contours of her features, with a soft glow from the sunlight filtering through the cockpit.
Glossy reflections from her cap, top, and accessories for a tactile, photorealistic finish.
The movement of the hair adds dynamic realism, with the breeze from the open cockpit slightly blowing it back and away from her face.

Negative Prompt: Do not render any cartoonish, anime, or stylized features. Avoid exaggerated proportions, flat shading, or unrealistic textures. Ensure the image remains photorealistic with natural human proportions, true-to-life lighting, and realistic textures.
```

## OOTD Magazine Fashion Collage Prompt (JSON/YAML)

> Minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "target": "NanoBanana",
  "role_task": "Create a professional OOTD magazine-style fashion collage",
  "layout": {
    "left": {
      "width_ratio": 0.60,
      "preserve": {
        "face": true,
        "hairstyle": true,
        "outfit": true
      },
      "photo_style": "clean, bright, magazine-quality",
      "pose": "cute, amusing, slightly quirky, realistic atmosphere"
    },
    "right": {
      "width_ratio": 0.40,
      "background": "white",
      "product_grid": {
        "quantity": "[quantity]",
        "arrangement": "vertical",
        "final_module": "underwear_estimated_from_image",
        "module_fields": [
          "product_image_cutout_white_bg",
          "product_name",
          "price_yen"
        ]
      }
    }
  },
  "example_products": [
    {"id":1, "name":"{argument name="Product 1 Name" default="[Product 1]"}", "price":"¥{argument name="Product 1 Price" default="[price]"}", "image":"[path_or_url_or_placeholder]"},
    {"id":2, "name":"{argument name="Product 2 Name" default="[Product 2]"}", "price":"¥{argument name="Product 2 Price" default="[price]"}", "image":"[path_or_url_or_placeholder]"},
    {"id":3, "name":"{argument name="Product 3 Name" default="[Product 3]"}", "price":"¥{argument name="Product 3 Price" default="[price]"}", "image":"[path_or_url_or_placeholder]"},
    {"id":4, "name":"{argument name="Product 4 Name" default="[Product 4]"}", "price":"¥{argument name="Product 4 Price" default="[price]"}", "image":"[path_or_url_or_placeholder]"},
    {"id":5, "name":"{argument name="Product 5 Name" default="[Product 5]"}", "price":"¥{argument name="Product 5 Price" default="[price]"}", "image":"[path_or_url_or_placeholder]"},
    {"id":6, "name":"{argument name="Product 6 Name" default="[Product 6] (final underwear)"}", "price":"¥{argument name="Product 6 Price" default="[price]"}", "image":"[path_or_url_or_placeholder]"}
  ],
  "typography": {
    "font_family": "modern_sans_serif",
    "product_name_weight": "medium",
    "price_weight": "bold"
  },
  "style": {
    "aesthetic": "bright, fresh, professional, Instagram fashion-blogger",
    "layout": "clean with generous white space",
    "color_palette": ["natural_tones", "white", "soft_gray"],
    "photo_quality": "professional_ecommerce_photography"
  },
  "output_requirements": {
    "file_format": "PNG or JPG at editorial resolution",
    "left_image_handling": "preserve original pixels for face/hair/outfit (no warp)",

A hyper-realistic, high-fashion photograph of a stylish young man in a modern industrial art gallery. The man has thick, messy dark hair styled in a voluminous quiff, a full groomed beard, and is wearing dark-tinted sunglasses. He is leaning casually against a massive, brushed-steel sculpture shaped like the letter 'Z'. Perched on top of the 'Z' structure are 3D metal letters spelling out "{argument name="brand name" default="ZarOon"}".
The man is dressed in trendy streetwear: an oversized t-shirt with a red, blue, and gold gradient tie-dye pattern featuring a "ZO" logo on the chest, paired with black cargo pants featuring orange and green vertical stripes on the pockets, and chunky white sneakers. He has his hands in his pockets and one leg crossed over the other.
The background is a minimalist gallery space with raw concrete walls (brutalist style) and polished concrete floors. Natural light streams in from a window on the right, creating soft shadows. In the background, there is another abstract twisted metal sculpture on a pedestal. 8k resolution, cinematic lighting, sharp focus, streetwear lookbook aesthetic.
```

## Ethereal Bloom Immersion Macro Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra realistic 8K extreme close up face portrait of a young woman with full facial alignment and identity preservation. Vertical 9:16 framing. No clothing is visible. Focus tightly on closed eyes, nose, and lips. Face slightly angled to the left side of the frame. Her face is partially submerged in a dark, rich rose-tinted liquid, nearly black beneath the surface, creating cinematic underwater shadowing. Wet black hair flows around the face with vivid neon pink tips reflecting softly in the water. Two hands gently touch each other near the side of her lips, interacting naturally with hair and skin in a neutral editorial manner. Surrounding the face, iridescent bubbles are artfully arranged to frame the face, creating layered depth and compositional interest. A small number of soft soap suds appear around select bubbles for texture. Hair strands float naturally, interacting with bubbles and light.

Mood and Concept:
Cinematic, immersive, and ethereal. Emphasis on editorial composition, layered textures, lighting, and color contrast. Extreme close-up highlights facial structure, bubble framing, and hair movement, with subtle leftward tilt of the face adding dynamic editorial composition.

Makeup and Nails:
Dewy porcelain skin with blush matching neon pink hair tips, applied on cheeks and temples for cohesive color harmony. Eyeshadow in soft pink-violet matching the hair tips, blended smoothly over closed eyelids. Gradient MLBB lips in a subtle rose tone. Fingernails in barely there tint with wet reflective quality.

Hair:
Wet black hair floating around the face with neon pink ends vividly visible and reflecting in the water. Strands interact naturally with bubbles and hands.

Accessories:
None. Only water, bubbles, soap suds, hair, and hands appear in frame.

Photography Specs:
Sony A1 with 90 mm macro lens
Aperture f2.0
Shutter speed 1/200 s
ISO 160
Vertical 9:16 extreme macro with controlled low key lighting and rich color saturation emphasizing underwater depth.

Detail and Finish:
High tactile realism in skin, hair, hands, and bubble membranes. Iridescent thin-film reflections on bubbles and soft soap suds create layered texture. Dark liquid base maintains cinematic shadow depth beneath hair. Neon pink hair tips and matching blush and eyeshadow reflect subtly in water. Wet surfaces preserve natural sheen. Bubbles are arranged to artistically frame the face, enhancing composition and focus. Hands touching near the side of the lips provide compositional balance. Final image reads as a refined, cinematic, editorial high-fashion beauty portrait with closed-eye serenity, subtle leftward angle, and cohesive pink color story.
```

## Nano Banana Pro Battle Entry Screen Prompt

> Minimal illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
layering:
  order:
    - text_layer
    - character_layer
    - background_layer
  strict: true

subject:
  description: >
    Preserve the subject exactly as in the reference image, keeping all facial features,
    colors, hairstyle, clothing, and proportions. No redesign or reinterpretation.
  framing: >
    Bust-up composition (head to upper torso), making the subject appear large and prominent.
  integrity: full_identity_preservation
  detail_level: ultra_high_resolution
  micro_details: preserve_skin_texture_hair_edges_fabric_details

composition:
  aspect_ratio: "16:9"
  layout: >
    Subject positioned on one side; headline text on the opposite side.
  emphasis: >
    Strong presence on the character's upper body and expression.

action:
  description: >
    Minimal motion enhancement. Maintain the reference pose while adding subtle dramatic energy.

location:
  description: >
    Generate a simple, abstract background inferred from the tone of the reference image.
    No specific elements required; allow the model to interpret color and atmosphere freely.
    Background must NOT contain any characters, silhouettes, faces, bodies,
    or blurred forms derived from the subject.
  background_feel: clean_and_minimal
  allowed_elements: pure_color_gradients_light_abstract_shapes_only
  prohibit_subject_reuse_in_background: true

style:
  visual_style: >
    High-impact reveal-splash aesthetic with strong contrast between character and headline.
  text_effects: >
    Metallic, high-energy lettering with pronounced depth and perspective. No glow effects.
  character_rendering: >
    Extremely high-resolution rendering with sharp facial details, clean hair definition,
    precise edges, and crisp texture fidelity.
  background_style_rules: >
    Background must be purely non-figurative. No silhouettes, no blurred shapes,
    and no reused subject elements.
  ink_splash_rules: >
    Ink splash must be pure black only. No other colors, tinting, blending, or variation.

camera_lighting:
  camera_angle: from_reference
  lighting: >
    Enhance with dramatic key lighting and subtle rim highlights to emphasize the reveal.
  clarity: maximum_definition

colors:
  palette: >
    Background colors inferred from the reference image.
    Character name in metallic silver; announcement text in saturated yellow.
  mood: dramatic, heroic, high_energy

text:
  character_name: "{argument name="character name" default="Character Name"}"
  announcement_fixed: "{argument name="announcement" default="Entering the Battle!!"}"
  placement: opposite_side_of_character
  perspective: >
    Pronounced perspective: front surface larger, back tapered with visible extrusion.
  character_name_style: >
    Metallic silver, b
```

Fragmented layout: main full-body frame plus three inset crops (face/drink, torso, pants cuff) linked by thin red vector lines. ## Glamorous Neon-Lit Cinematic Portrait

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "prompt": "A highly detailed, hyper-realistic, 8K portrait of a young woman seated elegantly on a modern staircase with a dark purple and pink neon-lit background. The subject is wearing a floor-length, figure-hugging, black evening gown with thin straps. Her pose is dynamic yet sophisticated, with one leg extended down the stairs and the other bent, drawing attention to her legs and the dress's slit. She is looking back over her shoulder at the camera with a subtle, engaging smile.\
\
**Outfit and Accessories:**\
* **Dress:** Black, sleek, backless, floor-length gown with a high slit, emphasizing an hourglass silhouette.\
* **Shoes:** High-heeled, gold, leopard-print ankle-wrap or gladiator sandals with thin straps that spiral up her calves, drawing focus to her legs.\
* **Jewelry:** Minimalist, perhaps small stud earrings.\
\
**Facial Features and Hair:**\
* **Face:** Youthful, defined features, light eye makeup (smoky or cat-eye), and a soft pink or nude lip color. She has bright, clear, captivating eyes.\
* **Hair:** Dark brown hair styled in a chic, messy high bun or topknot with a few soft strands framing her face.\
\
**Setting and Lighting:**\
* **Setting:** Indoors, on a set of wide, modern concrete or stone stairs. The steps are well-defined.\
* **Lighting:** Dramatic, high-contrast cinematic lighting. The background features a striking gradient of deep purples and pink/red neon lights, casting a dramatic glow on the scene. The key light emphasizes her skin tone and the texture of the dress.\
\
**Aesthetics:**\
* **Style:** Glamorous, fashion photography, high-end editorial.\
* **Focus:** Sharp focus on the subject, especially the face, skin, and shoes, with a shallow depth of field (bokeh) on the background lights.\
* **Quality:** Photorealistic, masterpiece, extremely detailed skin texture, intricate fabric detail, shot on a Sony A7R IV with a high-quality prime lens (e.g., 85mm f/1.4), cinematic color grading."
}
```

The lighting creates a sense of depth and highlights the contours of her body.",

{
  "meta_control": {

    "priority_stack": ["identity_lock", "perspective_physics", "material_fidelity", "environmental_coherence"],
    "quality_target": "editorial_print_ready"

  "subject": {
    "type": "Human female fashion model",
    "identity_lock": {
      "enforcement_level": "strict",

    "biometrics": {

## Hyper-Realistic Cinematic Portrait of a Captivating Woman in a Café

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
The same person, the same face, unaltered features. A medium shot, hyper-realistic, cinematographic, and professionally shot, of a captivating woman with fair skin, sleek, blond hair cascading over her shoulders, green eyes, and full lips painted with glossy red lipstick. Professional makeup with sharp details. She wears a tight, sheer black lace top and black velvet trousers, adorned with a delicate, shimmering gold heart pendant and matching thin gold earrings. A thin gold bracelet embellished with diamonds adorns her wrist. She sits at a wooden table in a café, her left hand on a silver laptop keyboard, a black sheet of paper in her right, gazing down with a serene expression. In front of her is a slice of sumptuous chocolate cake on a white plate and a glass of iced drink. In the background, a large window overlooks a grand building bearing the inscription "GRAND HOTEL ROSA," revealing the historic facade with its arched windows. Soft, natural light streams in from the window, creating a pleasant glow on the woman's face. The colors are warm, with an emphasis on shades of brown, black, and gold, and a shallow depth of field that focuses attention on the woman and what's in front of her.

negative_prompt: Low quality, blurry, distracting, blurry image, inaccurate details, unnatural colors.
```

## Vans Logo Street Fashion Portrait Replication

```
**THE T-SHIRT MUST DISPLAY A CLEAR, AUTHENTIC VANS LOGO (classic “Off The Wall” badge OR Vans wordmark) ON THE FRONT.**

The logo must appear naturally printed on the cropped mustard-yellow tee, following fabric curvature, folds, motion lift, lighting, and perspective exactly.

{
  "meta": {
    "title": "Walking Street Fashion – Matte Film Portrait (Vans Logo Variant)",
    "version": "1.0.1",
    "tags": [
      "portrait",
      "street-style",
      "walking-shot",
      "filmic",
      "vintage-matte",
      "fashion",
      "outdoor",
      "vans-logo"
    ]
  },
  "prompt_data": {
    "full_string": "Hyper-realistic 8K matte-filmic street portrait. Replace the woman with the uploaded subject while keeping the exact same walking pose, stride, arm swing, outfit structure, camera angle, lighting, background layout, and muted color grading. Scene: woman walking confidently down a quiet urban street, mid-stride. Body facing forward, torso upright, left leg stepping forward, right leg slightly behind. Arms swinging naturally with the motion—right arm extended back slightly, left arm slightly forward. Head facing forward with subtle hair movement. Expression calm, confident, neutral. Wardrobe: cropped {argument name="shirt color" default="mustard-yellow"} tee featuring a CLEAR, AUTHENTIC VANS LOGO printed on the chest; shirt is slightly loose and lifted by motion. High-waisted, bright {argument name="jeans color" default="blue"} distressed skinny jeans with extreme shredding across thighs and knees. Black-and-white Vans checkerboard slip-on shoes. Lighting: soft overcast daylight with smooth skin tones and low contrast shadows. Color grading: muted greens, matte yellows, subtle blues, vintage film texture, soft grain, slight vignette, Kodak Vision3/CineStill inspired tones. Background: parked cars along both sides, dark green chain-link fence on the left, tall muted trees, sidewalk and curb visible. Depth of field: medium-shallow—subject sharply focused, background softly blurred but clear enough to identify. Camera: eye-level, medium-full body portrait, telephoto-leaning focal length (70–105mm equivalent). Composition: subject centered, walking toward camera, shot straight down the street. The Vans logo MUST remain visible, well-proportioned, and realistically integrated into the tee’s fabric behavior. --ar 4:5 --style raw --v 6.0",
    "components": {
      "style": "Matte film-inspired street-style portrait with muted tones, soft grain, and low-contrast cinematic grading.",
      "subject": {
        "reference_instruction": "Use the uploaded subject’s identity while preserving the exact walking stride, arm movement, posture, and proportions.",
        "pose": "walking mid-stride toward the camera, torso upright, left leg forward, right leg back; arms swinging naturally; head facing forward with a relaxed, confident expression.",
        "clothing": {
          "top": "mustard-yellow cropped tee WITH A CLEAR VANS LOGO on the chest, integrated into folds and curvature.",
          "bottom": "high-waisted, bright blue distressed skinny jeans with extreme shredding across thighs and knees."
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
```

      },

      },

      "render_quality": {
        "resolution": "8k ultra fine detail",
        "post_process": "cinematic color grade, smooth noise, high clarity",

}

{

## Dramatic Neon Portrait with Wet Hair and Color Contrast

> Fütüristik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A mesmerizing, high-resolution studio portrait of a woman [image uploaded, no face alterations] with an ethereal and slightly enigmatic expression. Her medium-dark, straight hair is visibly wet, clinging in strands and creating a glistening texture, with some longer pieces cascading over her shoulders and down her back, while others frame her face. Her right hand (viewer's left) is raised, fingers splayed and gently intertwined within her wet hair, pushing it back from her forehead and crown, emphasizing the dampness and natural movement. Water droplets glisten on her skin, most noticeably on her forehead, cheekbones, and jawline, adding a tactile, lifelike quality to the scene.

The lighting is a sophisticated interplay of extreme color contrast. A vibrant, intense orange-red light illuminates the entire right side of her face (viewer's left), from her forehead down to her cheek, jawline, and neck, creating striking highlights on her skin and hair. This warm light also strongly catches the bridge of her nose, her right eye, and the contours of her lips. Conversely, the left side of her face (viewer's right) and shoulder are bathed in cooler, almost electric blue and green light, creating sharp shadows and further emphasizing the dramatic color separation. The interplay of these colors creates a strong visual division down the center of her face.

Her skin is impeccably smooth and dewy, with subtle sheen and natural texture visible, especially on her forehead, cheekbones, and lips, where the light reflects most intensely. Water droplets are strategically placed on her skin to amplify the tactile realism, glistening across the surface in contrast to the intense lighting. Her eyes are a prominent feature, dark brown or hazel, wide-set, and intensely focused on the camera, conveying a sense of directness and depth. The pupils are dilated, and the irises catch subtle reflections of the colorful lights, further adding to the realism. A small, bright catchlight appears in her eyes, adding a touch of life and sparkle. Her eyebrows are well-groomed and naturally arched.

Her lips are full and appear slightly parted, revealing just a hint of shadow between them, and are painted with a soft, natural reddish-pink hue that glows under the warm light. A single, small, silver stud earring is visible in her left earlobe (viewer's right). Her shoulders and upper chest are minimally visible, suggesting she might be wearing a simple, light-colored garment, possibly white or off-white, which peeks out subtly on her left side (viewer's right) as a soft, blurred shape.

The background is a smoothly transitioned, out-of-focus gradient of teal at the top left, shifting to a darker emerald green towards the bottom right, providing a minimalist yet impactful backdrop that complements the cool tones on her face and further isolates the subject.

The overall aesthetic is one of high fashion, artist
```

## Nano Banana Pro Chibi Snow Globe Prompt

> Minimal 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the uploaded image as the only character reference.
CONDITION
If the uploaded image is a real-life photo (live-action, not chibi/cartoon):
First convert the person into a 2-head-tall super-deformed chibi character.
Then render that chibi as a small cute 3D figure.
Keep the character’s key identity (face shape, hairstyle, hair color, outfit colors, accessories) clearly recognizable, just stylized in chibi form.
If the uploaded image is already a chibi / super-deformed / cartoon-style character:
Do not change the character’s face, facial expression, hairstyle, colors, or overall design.
Directly convert this exact design into a small cute 3D figure.
[SUBJECT]
small cute 3D figure style character based on the uploaded image,
big head, short body (2-head-tall chibi proportions),
toy-like look with realistic plastic texture,
single full-body character, centered in the frame
[SCENE]
place the character inside a glass snow globe,
miniature snowy town inside the dome,
tiny houses, street lights, Christmas decorations
[BACKGROUND]
Christmas night light bokeh,
dark blue night background with glowing lights
[STYLE]
realistic miniature photography,
macro lens look,
shallow depth of field,
soft warm lighting,
high detail, clean render
[EFFECT]
snow falling inside the globe,
light reflections on the glass surface,
natural shadows and highlights,
slightly whimsical, cozy Christmas mood
[POSE]
random cute pose that fits the character’s personality
[QUALITY]
sharp focus, high resolution, photorealistic background,
no text, no watermark, no extra characters
```

## 9-Shot Editorial Photo Grid Prompt for Nano Banana Pro

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Editorial 3x3 photo grid in a clean soft beige studio. 
Character (matches reference 100%) wearing lightweight dark navy shirt, ivory trousers, barefoot for raw simplicity. 
Lighting: large diffused key light directly front-right, silver reflector left, subtle rim from top. 
Shots to include: 1. extreme close-up of lips + cheekbone with blurred hand partially covering (85mm, f/1.8, razor-thin DOF); 
2. tight crop on eyes looking into lens with reflection of light strip visible (85mm, f/2.0); 
3. black & white close portrait resting chin on fist, face filling frame (50mm, f/2.2); 
4. over-shoulder shot, blurred foreground fabric curtain framing half face (85mm, f/2.0); 
5. very close frontal with hands overlapping face, light streak across eyes (50mm, f/2.5); 
6. tight angled portrait showing hair falling into eyes, soft-focus background (85mm, f/2.2); 
7. crop of hands touching jawline, eyes cropped out (50mm, f/3.2, detail-focused); 
8. half-body seated sideways on low cube, head turned sharply away, blurred foreground (35mm, f/ 4.5); 
9. intense close-up of profile with single tear-like water droplet, cinematic light slice across (85mm, f/ 1.9). 
Angles: mostly tight headshots with slight high/low tilts, maintaining variation. Capture RAW, professional muted grade, smooth tonal contrast, subtle cinematic grain. Mood: intimate, introspective, character-led editorial minimalism with delicate use of fabric as prop.
```

## Ultra-Wide Angle Dynamic Pose Remake Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra-Wide Angle Perspective and Dynamic Pose Remake Edit
This is an edit of the original image, not the generation of a new character.
Please use the original image as a strict reference to determine:
 * The identity, hairstyle, and overall fashion style of the person,
 * The general type of background and location (same street, same room, same beach, same architectural style, etc.).
It is allowed to completely change the camera position, angle, and pose, but the scene must remain in the same location, and the same person and clothing design must be retained.
Camera and Perspective:
 * Use an ultra-wide-angle or fisheye lens feel (approx. 12–18mm full-frame look).
 * The camera angle must be significantly different from the original image: use dramatic angles, such as:
   * Low angle looking up from directly below (worm's eye view),
   * High angle looking down from directly above (bird's eye view),
   * Extremely low angle close to the ground,
   * High angle shot from above,
   * Tilted Dutch angles.
 * Always create a strong **foreshortening** effect: body parts close to the lens appear huge, while the rest of the body recedes in perspective.
 * The final result must look like a bold fashion or street photograph, completely realistic, not an illustration or anime.
Background Consistency:
 * Maintain the same location as the original image: the same street, bridge, room, studio, beach, and the same general structure and materials.
 * Do not replace the background with a completely different place.
 * Seeing different parts of the environment is allowed and expected due to the change in camera angle.
 * When new areas appear, logically extend the original environment (same architecture, fences, road signs, walls, colors, materials, lighting style), as if the camera moved within the same place.
Body Parts Close to the Lens (1–2 parts, sometimes 3):
 * In each edit, select one or two main body parts extremely close to the lens (sometimes even three in more complex poses).
 * Vary between different images; do not always use the same body parts.
 * Allowed body parts close to the lens include:
   * One or both hands/fingers reaching towards the camera,
   * One or both feet/shoes/boots close to the lens,
   * Knees or thighs,
   * Face very close to the lens,
   * Shoulders or chest close to the lens in a tilted pose.
 * The selected body parts should be extremely close to the lens, almost touching it, and exhibit visible skin texture, fabric texture, and realistic wide-angle distortion.
Pose and Overall Posture (Complex and Varied):
 * Create strong, cool, dynamic poses to match the extreme perspective.
 * Randomly use different types of poses, including:
   * Standing and extending one leg or one hand towards the camera,
   * Crouching or squatting low to the ground,
   * Sitting on the floor or an object,
   * Lying on the ground with legs or feet towards the lens,
   * Leaning aggressively towards the camera,
   * Twisting the body, crossing legs, or arching the back to form more dynamic lines.
 * Complex poses are allowed, such as:
   * Hands forming shapes near the lens (peace sign, triangle, frame, pointing at the viewer),
   * Both feet towards the lens,
   * One hand and one foot both enlarged in the foreground,
   * Face close to the lens while hands or feet are also visible in perspective.
 * Maintain credible anatomy even with extreme foreshortening.
Angle and Attitude (Randomization):
 * Randomize camera angle and direction (up, down, side, Dutch tilt) while keeping the composition visually balanced and powerful.
 * Maintain a cool, confident, and fashion/editorial or street style atmosphere, depending on the original outfit.
 * Facial expressions can vary (serious, playful, confident, mysterious) but must still look like the same person.
Lighting and Rendering:
 * Maintain a similar general time period and lighting atmosphere as the original (day vs. night, indoor vs. outdoor, soft light vs. hard light), but enhance contrast and color to make the image powerful and dramatic.
 * Maintain realistic shadows and contact points with the ground or floor.
 * High resolution, sharp details, with clear skin texture, fabric weave, and material highlights.
Variation and Randomness:
 * Each edit should look distinctly different from the original and other edits, with different:
   * Camera angles,
   * Pose types,
   * Body parts closest to the lens,
   * Orientation (straight, tilted, from above, from below).
 * Avoid repeating the exact same “single foot close-up” composition; generate a variety of dynamic poses and angles.
Strict Rules:
 * Do not turn the person into someone else.
 * Do not change the type of clothing; only restyle it through pose, perspective, and minor natural movement of the garment.
 * Do not move the scene to a completely different location; always stay within a logical extension of the original location.
 * Do not add text, logos, watermarks, or graphic design elements.
 * Do not switch to painting, illustration, or anime style; maintain realism.
Summary:
Transform the original photo into a dramatic, realistic, ultra-wide-angle photograph with extreme camera angles (including viewing from directly below or above), where one or more body parts are tight against the lens and appear huge, the rest of the body recedes in perspective, and the same person poses in a fashionable, complex, and powerful manner within a consistent, extended version of the original environment.
```

## Golden Hour Luxury Sports Car Portrait Prompt with Face Lock

> Fotogerçekçi, lüks mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Edit the image, ensuring the face, hair, and clothes for BOTH the woman and the man are kept 100% identical to their respective uploaded images. These elements are locked and must not be changed or edited in any way. **Subjects** * **The Woman**: As described below, with face, hair, and clothes from her uploaded image. * **The Man**: He is the driver. His face, hair, and clothes should be kept 100% identical to a reference image. He has a relaxed and calm demeanor that complements the woman's style. **Realism & Photographic Details** * **Photography Style**: Ultra-realistic, photorealistic, 8K, RAW photo. * **Camera & Lens**: Shot on a 35mm film camera with a 50mm f/1.4 lens. Introduce subtle lens distortion and very slight chromatic aberration on the edges of high-contrast areas. * **Film & Grain**: Emulate the look of Portra 400 film with fine, natural film grain. Add halation, a soft red glow around highlights. * **Focus & Sharpeness**: Use a shallow depth of field (f/1.4) to create a soft, blurry background (bokeh). The focus should be critically sharp on the woman's eyes, with a natural fall-off. The man will be slightly less in focus. The overall image should not be artificially sharpened. * **Texture & Micro-details**: Render hyper-realistic skin texture on the woman, including visible pores, fine lines, and nearly invisible vellus hair (peach fuzz). Fabric textures on the car interior should be highly detailed. Capture subtle imperfections like dust motes in the air, fingerprints on the car window, and minor reflections on the glossy lips. **Lighting & Mood** * **Source & Quality**: Natural, soft-diffused sunlight from the "golden hour" (late afternoon) streaming through the car's side window (passenger side). * **Effect**: The light should cast soft shadows and create a warm, gentle glow primarily on the right side of the woman's face. The man, in the driver's seat, will be more in shadow, creating depth and dimension in the scene. Create specular highlights on the woman's cheekbones and glossy lips. * **Contrast**: The car's interior should be in shadow, creating a high-contrast scene that makes the subjects pop. **Pose & Composition** * **Scene**: A candid, stylish moment inside a **luxury sports car, open roof type** featuring two people. * **Woman's Pose**: She is in the passenger seat, leaning forward slightly, resting her chin on one hand in a candid, thoughtful, or slightly bored manner. * **Man's Pose**: He is in the driver's seat, relaxed, with one hand resting on the steering wheel. He is looking forward, focused on the road, or taking a quick, natural glance toward the woman. * **Angle**: Shot from a slightly low angle from the passenger side. * **Framing**: The woman is the primary subject in the foreground. Include her knees/legs to enhance the candid, casual feel. The man is partially framed, visible from the chest up. **Woman's Details** * **Expression**: A neutral, calm, confide
```

## Anti-Plastic/Hyper-Realism Protocol for Human Skin Texture

> Fotogerçekçi görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
When generating images of humans: use ANTI-PLASTIC/HYPER-REALISM protocol. All images must be EVER in 8K resolution. OVERRIDE default smoothing. Render skin as biological topography.

[1] ADAPTIVE SETUP: Adapt Subject/Light/Style to query. SCALING: Close-Up->Force Macro/Micro-details. Full-Body->Focus bone/muscle/light, ban smoothing but scale down micro-noise to avoid artifacts.

[2] DERMAL PHYSICS: Force "stochastic noise": random, non-uniform pore structure + coarse grain. Ban smooth/waxy surfaces. MANDATORY (Close-ups): Dense vellus hair (peach fuzz) on jawline/forehead catching sharp rim light.

[3] SUBSURFACE SCATTERING: Reject uniform tones. Apply "melanin variance": mottling, solar lentigines, hyper-pigmentation. Simulate thin-skin translucency. Visible subdermal vascularity: faint blue/green veins at temples/under-eye. Erythema + telangiectasia around nostrils/tear ducts.

[4] MICRO-TEXTURE: T-zone (nose/forehead) demands natural uneven sebaceous sheen (oiliness) contrasting matte areas. Highlights must reveal skin micro-relief

[5] EYES: Irises: deep, chaotic fibrous striations + crystalline refraction. Sclera: distinct red vascular mapping. Lower waterline: thick, pooling liquid reflection (tear film)

[6] LIPS: Texturally dry/dehydrated. Deep vertical fissures, micro-cracks, peeling flakes. Uneven specular highlights

[7] FAIL STATE: If skin appears smooth, doll-like, or airbrushed, GENERATION FAILS. Maximize texture defects, asymmetry, topographical imperfections
```
